{"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Q25VoZCLF9UPNYugn2M44J","type":"tool_result","content":"import Mathlib\nimport PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.SolderedSquareGram\n\n/-!\n# Move-2 IDENTIFICATION - the aperture block `Q_A` IS the invariant mass `Q(∑ α)`\n\nThe first Move-2 identification lemma: it ties the abstract aperture block `Q_A` of the\nWeitzenböck decomposition to the **aperture functional** (the invariant mass of a sum of\nnull momenta), which is the actual physics content the `D²` decomposition was scaffolding.\n\nWith unit weights (`x ≡ 1`), the null-soldered square is the scalar image of the quadratic\nform evaluated on the **total** soldering:\n\n>   `(∑ₑ c(αₑ))² = algebraMap R _ (Q (∑ₑ αₑ))`.\n\nBecause `(∑ₑ c(αₑ))² = Q_A` (brick 2a, `nullSoldered_square_isScalar`: with unit weights the\nsoldered square is exactly the aperture Gram scalar, `Q_C` absent), this says\n**`Q_A = Q(∑ₑ αₑ)`** — the aperture block equals `minkowskiSq` of the summed null momenta.\nHence `Q_A = 0` iff the total soldering is null, which — in the Minkowski specialization\n`Q = minkowskiSq`, `αₑ = ` future-null momenta — is exactly the landed aperture theorem\n`NBodyAperture.nbody_aperture_massless_iff_collinear` (`minkowskiSq (∑ pᵢ) = 0 ↔` collinear).\n\nSo the chain is: abstract `Q_A` block  =  `Q(∑ α)`  =  aperture invariant mass, and its\nvanishing locus is the collinear locus. This is the honest identification the decomposition\nneeded; the `Q_A` naming is now a theorem, not a convention.\n\n## Scope / honesty (draft)\n\nOver a field `R` (so `algebraMap` into the Clifford algebra is injective and `Q_A = 0 ↔\nQ(∑α) = 0`). The bridge to `NBodyAperture` is stated as a docstring correspondence here and\nmade a literal `↔` only in the Minkowski specialization (a follow-up, since `NBodyAperture`\nuses the concrete `Momentum4`/`minkowskiSq` API rather than an abstract `QuadraticForm`).\nProvenance: corollary of brick 2a (`SolderedSquareGram`); Move-2 of the carrier program.\n\nProof handoff (for Aristotle):\n- `Q_A_eq_totalSq`: from `nullSoldered_square_eq_algebraMap Q alpha (fun _ => 1) h2`\n  (brick 2a) the square is `algebraMap (2⁻¹ * ∑ₑ ∑_f (1*1) * polar Q αₑ α_f)`. Simplify the\n  scalar: `∑ₑ ∑_f polar Q αₑ α_f = polar Q (∑ₑ αₑ) (∑_f α_f)` by bilinearity of `polar`\n  (`QuadraticMap.polar_sum` / `map_sum` on each slot), `= polar Q (∑α) (∑α) = 2 • Q (∑α)`\n  (`QuadraticMap.polar_self`), so `2⁻¹ * (2 * Q(∑α)) = Q(∑α)`.\n- `Q_A_zero_iff`: `algebraMap` is injective over a field (`algebraMap_eq_zero_iff` /\n  `NoZeroDivisors` + `FaithfulSMul`), so the square is `0` iff `Q (∑ₑ αₑ) = 0`.\n-/\n\nopen scoped BigOperators\n\nnamespace PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier\n\nvariable {R V : Type*} [Field R] [AddCommGroup V] [Module R V]\n\n/-- **Move-2 aperture identification (core).**  With unit weights, the null-soldered square\nis the scalar image of the quadratic form on the total soldering: `(∑ₑ c(αₑ))² =\nalgebraMap R _ (Q (∑ₑ αₑ))`.  Since the LHS is the aperture block `Q_A` (brick 2a), this is\n`Q_A = Q(∑ₑ αₑ)` — the aperture block equals the invariant mass of the summed null momenta. -/\ntheorem Q_A_eq_totalSq {E : Type*} [Fintype E] (Q : QuadraticForm R V) (alpha : E → V)\n    (h2 : (2 : R) ≠ 0) :\n    nullSoldered Q alpha (fun _ => 1) ^ 2\n      = algebraMap R (CliffordAlgebra Q) (Q (∑ e, alpha e)) := by\n  rw [nullSoldered_square_eq_algebraMap Q alpha (fun _ => 1) h2]\n  congr 1\n  -- The double sum of the polar form is the polar of the total soldering.\n  have hbil : (∑ e, ∑ f, QuadraticMap.polar (⇑Q) (alpha e) (alpha f))\n      = QuadraticMap.polar (⇑Q) (∑ e, alpha e) (∑ f, alpha f) := by\n    conv_rhs => rw [← QuadraticMap.polarBilin_apply_apply]\n    simp only [map_sum, LinearMap.sum_apply, QuadraticMap.polarBilin_apply_apply]\n    rw [Finset.sum_comm]\n  simp only [one_mul, mul_one]\n  rw [hbil, QuadraticMap.polar_self, nsmul_eq_mul, Nat.cast_ofNat, ← mul_assoc,\n    inv_mul_cancel₀ h2, one_mul]\n\n/-- **The aperture block vanishes iff the total soldering is null.**  `Q_A = 0 ↔\nQ(∑ₑ αₑ) = 0` — the carrier form of \"the composite is massless iff the total momentum is\nnull\", whose collinear characterization is `NBodyAperture.nbody_aperture_massless_iff_collinear`\nin the Minkowski specialization. -/\ntheorem Q_A_zero_iff_totalSq_zero {E : Type*} [Fintype E] (Q : QuadraticForm R V)\n    (alpha : E → V) (h2 : (2 : R) ≠ 0) :\n    nullSoldered Q alpha (fun _ => 1) ^ 2 = 0 ↔ Q (∑ e, alpha e) = 0 := by\n  rw [Q_A_eq_totalSq Q alpha h2]\n  haveI : Invertible (2 : R) := invertibleOfNonzero h2\n  constructor","is_error":false}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e6c6e3b0-0265-4770-b1a6-d84ed8d06188","uuid":"2bba54e3-472c-4e2a-96ef-ca1e53c7a1e7","timestamp":"2026-07-08T07:37:13.617Z","tool_use_result":{"stdout":"import Mathlib\nimport PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier.SolderedSquareGram\n\n/-!\n# Move-2 IDENTIFICATION - the aperture block `Q_A` IS the invariant mass `Q(∑ α)`\n\nThe first Move-2 identification lemma: it ties the abstract aperture block `Q_A` of the\nWeitzenböck decomposition to the **aperture functional** (the invariant mass of a sum of\nnull momenta), which is the actual physics content the `D²` decomposition was scaffolding.\n\nWith unit weights (`x ≡ 1`), the null-soldered square is the scalar image of the quadratic\nform evaluated on the **total** soldering:\n\n>   `(∑ₑ c(αₑ))² = algebraMap R _ (Q (∑ₑ αₑ))`.\n\nBecause `(∑ₑ c(αₑ))² = Q_A` (brick 2a, `nullSoldered_square_isScalar`: with unit weights the\nsoldered square is exactly the aperture Gram scalar, `Q_C` absent), this says\n**`Q_A = Q(∑ₑ αₑ)`** — the aperture block equals `minkowskiSq` of the summed null momenta.\nHence `Q_A = 0` iff the total soldering is null, which — in the Minkowski specialization\n`Q = minkowskiSq`, `αₑ = ` future-null momenta — is exactly the landed aperture theorem\n`NBodyAperture.nbody_aperture_massless_iff_collinear` (`minkowskiSq (∑ pᵢ) = 0 ↔` collinear).\n\nSo the chain is: abstract `Q_A` block  =  `Q(∑ α)`  =  aperture invariant mass, and its\nvanishing locus is the collinear locus. This is the honest identification the decomposition\nneeded; the `Q_A` naming is now a theorem, not a convention.\n\n## Scope / honesty (draft)\n\nOver a field `R` (so `algebraMap` into the Clifford algebra is injective and `Q_A = 0 ↔\nQ(∑α) = 0`). The bridge to `NBodyAperture` is stated as a docstring correspondence here and\nmade a literal `↔` only in the Minkowski specialization (a follow-up, since `NBodyAperture`\nuses the concrete `Momentum4`/`minkowskiSq` API rather than an abstract `QuadraticForm`).\nProvenance: corollary of brick 2a (`SolderedSquareGram`); Move-2 of the carrier program.\n\nProof handoff (for Aristotle):\n- `Q_A_eq_totalSq`: from `nullSoldered_square_eq_algebraMap Q alpha (fun _ => 1) h2`\n  (brick 2a) the square is `algebraMap (2⁻¹ * ∑ₑ ∑_f (1*1) * polar Q αₑ α_f)`. Simplify the\n  scalar: `∑ₑ ∑_f polar Q αₑ α_f = polar Q (∑ₑ αₑ) (∑_f α_f)` by bilinearity of `polar`\n  (`QuadraticMap.polar_sum` / `map_sum` on each slot), `= polar Q (∑α) (∑α) = 2 • Q (∑α)`\n  (`QuadraticMap.polar_self`), so `2⁻¹ * (2 * Q(∑α)) = Q(∑α)`.\n- `Q_A_zero_iff`: `algebraMap` is injective over a field (`algebraMap_eq_zero_iff` /\n  `NoZeroDivisors` + `FaithfulSMul`), so the square is `0` iff `Q (∑ₑ αₑ) = 0`.\n-/\n\nopen scoped BigOperators\n\nnamespace PhysicsSM.Draft.NullEdge.Carrier\n\nvariable {R V : Type*} [Field R] [AddCommGroup V] [Module R V]\n\n/-- **Move-2 aperture identification (core).**  With unit weights, the null-soldered square\nis the scalar image of the quadratic form on the total soldering: `(∑ₑ c(αₑ))² =\nalgebraMap R _ (Q (∑ₑ αₑ))`.  Since the LHS is the aperture block `Q_A` (brick 2a), this is\n`Q_A = Q(∑ₑ αₑ)` — the aperture block equals the invariant mass of the summed null momenta. -/\ntheorem Q_A_eq_totalSq {E : Type*} [Fintype E] (Q : QuadraticForm R V) (alpha : E → V)\n    (h2 : (2 : R) ≠ 0) :\n    nullSoldered Q alpha (fun _ => 1) ^ 2\n      = algebraMap R (CliffordAlgebra Q) (Q (∑ e, alpha e)) := by\n  rw [nullSoldered_square_eq_algebraMap Q alpha (fun _ => 1) h2]\n  congr 1\n  -- The double sum of the polar form is the polar of the total soldering.\n  have hbil : (∑ e, ∑ f, QuadraticMap.polar (⇑Q) (alpha e) (alpha f))\n      = QuadraticMap.polar (⇑Q) (∑ e, alpha e) (∑ f, alpha f) := by\n    conv_rhs => rw [← QuadraticMap.polarBilin_apply_apply]\n    simp only [map_sum, LinearMap.sum_apply, QuadraticMap.polarBilin_apply_apply]\n    rw [Finset.sum_comm]\n  simp only [one_mul, mul_one]\n  rw [hbil, QuadraticMap.polar_self, nsmul_eq_mul, Nat.cast_ofNat, ← mul_assoc,\n    inv_mul_cancel₀ h2, one_mul]\n\n/-- **The aperture block vanishes iff the total soldering is null.**  `Q_A = 0 ↔\nQ(∑ₑ αₑ) = 0` — the carrier form of \"the composite is massless iff the total momentum is\nnull\", whose collinear characterization is `NBodyAperture.nbody_aperture_massless_iff_collinear`\nin the Minkowski specialization. -/\ntheorem Q_A_zero_iff_totalSq_zero {E : Type*} [Fintype E] (Q : QuadraticForm R V)\n    (alpha : E → V) (h2 : (2 : R) ≠ 0) :\n    nullSoldered Q alpha (fun _ => 1) ^ 2 = 0 ↔ Q (∑ e, alpha e) = 0 := by\n  rw [Q_A_eq_totalSq Q alpha h2]\n  haveI : Invertible (2 : R) := invertibleOfNonzero h2\n  constructor","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false}}
{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011CcpCYjGmLKyiv3ENdCVrF","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01NTcJ2NbEkoRXKxVQLj5bqd","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"python -c \"\nfrom fractions import Fraction as F\nimport itertools\ndef M(rows): return [[F(x) for x in r] for r in rows]\ndef mul(A,B):\n    n=len(A); m=len(B[0]); k=len(B)\n    return [[sum(A[i][t]*B[t][j] for t in range(k)) for j in range(m)] for i in range(n)]\ndef add(A,B): return [[A[i][j]+B[i][j] for j in range(len(A[0]))] for i in range(len(A))]\ndef sub(A,B): return [[A[i][j]-B[i][j] for j in range(len(A[0]))] for i in range(len(A))]\ndef tr(A): return sum(A[i][i] for i in range(len(A)))\ndef T(A): return [list(r) for r in zip(*A)]\nu=M([[F(3,5),F(-4,5),0],[F(4,5),F(3,5),0],[0,0,1]])\nv=M([[1,0,0],[0,F(3,5),F(-4,5)],[0,F(4,5),F(3,5)]])\nw=M([[F(3,5),0,F(-4,5)],[0,1,0],[F(4,5),0,F(3,5)]])\nuv=mul(u,v); vu=mul(v,u)\nM1=tr(mul(uv,T(w))); M2=tr(mul(vu,T(w)))\nprint('Tr(uv w^T)=',M1,' Tr(vu w^T)=',M2)\nevQC = 2*(M1-M2)\nevQA = 2*(M1+M2)\nevQT4 = 4*3  # 4 * ||psi||^2, phi=1, ||chi||^2=1, ||s||^2=3\nMsq = evQA+evQC+evQT4\nprint('ev(QC)=',evQC,'ev(QA)=',evQA,'4ev(QT)=',evQT4,'M2=',Msq)\nprint('bA=',evQA/Msq,'bC=',evQC/Msq,'bT=',evQT4/Msq, 'sum=',evQA/Msq+evQC/Msq+evQT4/Msq)\n\"\n","description":"Verify singlet witness budget fractions with exact rationals"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1690,"cache_read_input_tokens":67328,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1690,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":3,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"e6c6e3b0-0265-4770-b1a6-d84ed8d06188","uuid":"aca1ed26-e660-4da5-aa03-b832b6554b2a"}

Mismatches found, all minor but real:\n\n1. **`anticonj_trace_zero` docstring still says \"Even-power form\"** for what is the `k = 0` *odd* case (`Tr B¹`). Flagged in call-02, still unfixed at line 92 of `S1CCBalancedInertia.lean`. Handoff stands.\n2. **`witness_budget_sum_one` vs manuscript §4**: the kernel pins *sum = 1* and *Q_C = 0* (`witness_QC_zero`); the individual values `(1/2, 0, 1/2)` are derivable from `hDD` but are **not separately stated theorems**. The manuscript's \"budget `(1/2, 0, 1/2)` (`witness_budget_sum_one`, M)\" is a hair of docstring-outruns-kernel. Fix: either add `witness_bA_half` or soften to \"with closure share exactly 0 (kernel-pinned) and shares `(1/2,0,1/2)` (arithmetic consequence)\".\n3. **`aperture_dominance_pos`**: sound as stated; two instantiation traps. In particular `g = 1` gives `tr Γ|_P = ind(X|_P)` (= graded dimension), and `dim(ker X ∩ P) ≥ |L(1)|`.\n- **(iii) Sector pinning (multiplicative face).** If `Γ` is an odd involution (`ΓWΓ = W†`) and `R` an even involution commuting with both, then `C := ΓW` is an involution, and in each `R`-sector `V_χ` the integers `ν₀(χ) = ½ tr((Γ + ΓW)P_χ)`, `ν_π(χ) = ½ tr((Γ − ΓW)P_χ)` satisfy `dim(ker(W∓1) ∩ V_χ) ≥ |ν(χ)|`; when `tr(ΓW P_χ)` collapses (half-winding case) the value is the fixed-point count `±¼ tr(ΓR)` — a Lefschetz number not involving `W`, whence amplitude-independence.\n\n**The unifying observation, stated plainly:** the program discovered three gradings separately — the chirality `Γ`, the closure bivector `b = σ_z ⊗ 1`, and the edge-orientation-reversal `Γ` of the GW structure — and they are all **odd elements of one group `Ĝ` acting on the decorated complex**, while gauge transformations and the reflection `R` are even elements. This single lemma *is* clause (ii); `g = 1` re-derives the index protection family.\n- **L4** `sector_involution_pinning` — **M-target**, exactly the C4-doc spec (`Submodule.finrank_sup_add_finrank_inf_eq`; no spectral theorem). Grade of the framework claim itself: **MEMO** until L2–L4 land; the claim \"RG-Schur is not an instance\" is **MEMO** (structural argument, above).\n\n---\n\n## Part B — the S6 color-singlet mass-budget witness (concrete design)\n\n**First, a no-go that must shape the design (this is the key finding of this part).** The ask's \"single-fiber singlet\" (`Λ⁰ ⊕ Λ³` of one `Λ(ℂ³)`) provably gives `b_C = 0` for *any* transports acting through the color representation: the singlet line has **multiplicity one**, so (Schur) every invariant operator acts there as a scalar; and for one-leg background transports the singlet expectation functional is the normalized trace, which kills the commutator `K`. Same for a maximally-entangled meson with covariant pair-transport (`v ⊗ v̄`): conjugation-invariance of trace kills it. I verified both vanishings by exact arithmetic this call. **Corollary worth landing as its own mini-theorem** (`singlet_one_leg_closure_zero`, M-target): `b_C ≠ 0` requires the singlet to *stretch over a non-flat holonomy loop*. This makes the witness principled, not tuned — the closure share of a singlet is literally a **difference of Wilson loops**, zero on flat backgrounds.\n\n### The witness (all entries rational; verified by exact-fraction oracle this call)\n\n**Space** `V = ℂ² ⊗ (ℂ³ ⊗ ℂ̄³)`, dim 18, indexed `Fin 2 × (Fin 3 × Fin 3)` (spinor ⊗ quark ⊗ antiquark).\n\n**Operators** (Kronecker products; `1ₖ` = identity):\n\n- Clifford null pair: `γ₁ = E₀₁ ⊗ 1₉`, `γ₂ = E₁₀ ⊗ 1₉`; metric `g = !![0,1;1,0]` (checks `hcl`: `γᵢ² = 0`, `{γ₁,γ₂} = 1`).\n- Grading `Γ = σ_z ⊗ 1₉`; turn `φ = 1₁₈` (so `hGammaSq`, `hGammaAnti`, `hPhiGamma`, `hPhiComm`, `hCov` are immediate).\n- Transports (3-4-5 rational rotations `u = R_z`, `v = R_x`, `w = R_y`, e.g. `u = (1/5)!![3,−4,0; 4,3,0; 0,0,5]`):\n  `∇₁ = 1₂ ⊗ (u ⊗ 1₃)` (edge 1 moves the quark), `∇₂ = 1₂ ⊗ (v ⊗ w̄)` (edge 2 moves both ends along *different* links — `w̄ = w` since real). Non-commuting: `K = [∇₁,∇₂] = 1₂ ⊗ ([u,v] ⊗ w) ≠ 0`, so `Q_C = 2 σ_z ⊗ ([u,v] ⊗ w) ≠ 0`.\n\n**State** `ψ = χ ⊗ s`, `χ = (1,0)` (spin-up), `s = Σₐ eₐ ⊗ fₐ` (unnormalized ε-singlet; normalization cancels in shares). `ev = ⟨ψ, ·ψ⟩` (Hilbert functional — see trap 3).\n\n**Predicted budget** (exact; key identity `⟨s|A⊗B|s⟩ = Tr(A Bᵀ)`):\n\n```text\nev(Q_C) = 2·Tr([u,v]wᵀ) = 2·(71/125 − 199/125) = −256/125   (a Wilson-loop difference)\nev(Q_A) = 2·Tr((uv+vu)wᵀ) = 540/125\n4·ev(Q_T) = 4·‖ψ‖² = 12\nM² = 1784/125,   (b_A, b_C, b_T) = (135/446, −32/223, 375/446),  sum = 1 ✓\n```\n\n**b_C ≠ 0 and negative** — the balanced-closure sign structure, exhibited. Bonus prediction: the spin-down state `χ = (0,1)` flips the closure sign: `(b_A, b_C, b_T) = (135/574, +32/287, 375/574)` with `M²(↓) − M²(↑) = 512/125 > 0` — an honest finite **hyperfine (π/ρ-analog) splitting**, the spin-aligned/anti-aligned pair split by the chromomagnetic channel alone. Land both states plus the flat negative control (`∇₂ = 1₂ ⊗ (v ⊗ v̄)` ⇒ `ev(Q_C) = 0` exactly).\n\n**Convention traps (flag all five in the module docstring):**\n\n1. **`Λ(ℂ³)` vs `ℂ³⊗ℂ̄³`**: the antiquark leg is `Λ²(ℂ³)` via the ε/Hodge map — sign trap `⋆e₂ = e₃∧e₁` (not `e₁∧e₃`), and `Λ²(g) ≅ ḡ` only for `det g = 1`. The two-fiber realization above is the safe concrete model.\n2. **XOR-Fano**: do *not* build the singlet from octonion basis products; Fano orientation signs differ from wedge signs. Strand-count only (`{e001,e010,e100} = Λ¹` etc.); anything octonionic goes through `ConventionBridge`.\n3. **Krein `#` vs Hilbert `†`**: `ev` here is Hilbert. The Krein functional `⟨ψ, J·ψ⟩` with `J = σ_z⊗1` (which *is* the closure bivector — Part A again) coincides for spin-up but not in general; the docstring must name the functional, and prose must not call `ev(D²)` \"the mass\" (see Part C).\n4. **Real transports don't probe `3` vs `3̄`**: with complex `SU(3)` entries the antiquark factor is `w̄` and the color trace is `Tr([u,v]w†)` — keep the conjugate explicit.\n5. **Proof route**: discharge `carrier_square_assembly` hypotheses structurally via Kronecker lemmas (`Matrix.kroneckerMap` API), evaluating only 3×3 traces by `norm_num` — do not `fin_cases` an 18×18 matrix.\n\nGrades: hypothesis discharge + budget values = **M-target** (rational `decide`/`norm_num`; numbers oracle-verified this call, so currently **C** with the kill condition \"exact Lean arithmetic disagrees with `(135/446, −32/223, 375/446)`\"); the one-leg/multiplicity-one no-go = **M-target**; the hyperfine reading = **MEMO**.\n\n---\n\n## Part C — the single weakest load-bearing link\n\n**It is the mass functional.** Not `Q_A` vs `det P` (that weld is further along than the prompt suggests: `Q_A_eq_totalSq` already pins `Q_A = Q(Σα)` at unit transports, with a specced lane to `NBodyAperture`); not channel canonicity (the split is symmetry-typed — symmetric vs antisymmetric under `e↔f`, plus the scalar turn — a referee can be answered); not finite-to-continuum (explicitly disclaimed). The vulnerability is that **\"mass\" appears in the manuscript with two non-identified meanings**: in §3 mass is `det P` of a state's momentum (spectral/invariant, trusted); from §4 on, \"mass\" is `M² := 4·ev(D²)` — the expectation of an operator square in a *chosen* state against a *chosen* linear functional. Every headline downstream (budget shares, the S6 witness above, the hyperfine splitting, the Banks–Casher reading) uses the expectation meaning while borrowing prestige from the determinant meaning. A sharp referee will say: *an expectation of `D²` is a mass only at an eigenstate; on a Krein space it is not even variationally meaningful without positivity — so the four-channel budget decomposes a quadratic functional, not a mass.* That is the deepest conceptual hole, and both §4 rails leave it open.\n\n**The one shoring-up theorem** — and the just-landed `aperture_dominance_pos` makes it ripe:\n\n> **`sector_ground_mass` (finite Rayleigh–Ritz keystone, M-target).** Hypotheses: a sector `P` that is `J`-positive-definite and on which the dominance inequality of `aperture_dominance_pos` holds for `J·(Q_A+Q_C)` (trap 3(a) above), with `D` Krein-self-adjoint. Conclusions: (a) `D^#D|_P` is diagonalizable with real spectrum bounded below by `c − κ > 0` in the `J`-inner product; (b) `M²_spec := min spec(D^#D|_P) = min_{ψ∈P∖0} 4·ev_ψ(D²)/‖ψ‖²_J`; (c) at a minimizer `ψ₀`, the signed budget shares decompose `M²_spec`.\n\nOne theorem, three payoffs: it converts `ev(D²)` from a chosen functional into the spectral mass of the sector ground state (the referee's objection dies exactly there); it gives the budget its referee-proof sentence (\"the *ground-state* mass decomposes into signed channel shares\"); and it is precisely the precondition S5 (first-meson) needs, connecting to the OS/transfer-gap pillars so the same number has an operational strong-coupling meaning.
